# Claim inventory — every buyer-facing assertion, classified

Generated 2026-08-21 from a full-surface extraction pass (9 agents, one per surface
group, plus an adversarial completeness critic), then corrected by hand against
the critic's findings. **This inventory records and classifies claims; it does
not rewrite copy** — backlog row 6 executes the rewrite against it. The
machine-readable twin is [`docs/agent/CLAIM_INVENTORY.json`](agent/CLAIM_INVENTORY.json)
(same rows, same count).

## How the surface list was derived (re-derivable)

The scan set is the union of the public claim surfaces the existing gates
already enumerate — not a hand-picked list, so a new page cannot be silently
omitted (the failure `scripts/launch-profile.mjs:84-90` records for app-surfaces):

- `README.md` plus every file under `artifacts/signalgrid-web/src/pages/` and
  `artifacts/signalgrid-web/src/components/sections/` (the docs-sanity
  SCAN_PATHS web tree that carries buyer-facing prose);
- `docs/fabric-console.html` and `docs/room-entry-console.html` (the two
  self-contained consoles README points buyers at).

Re-derive with: `ls artifacts/signalgrid-web/src/pages artifacts/signalgrid-web/src/components/sections docs/*console*.html`
and compare against the per-surface sections below.

## Snapshot semantics

The inventory records the tree **as of its extraction commit**; a claim this
same PR removes or rewrites keeps its row — marked **✔ RESOLVED in this PR** —
because the row is the evidence the correction answered. Rows without a
resolution marker describe the checked-in surfaces. The source↔inventory
synchronization gate (a stale row must fail, a new claim must fail) is
backlog row 6's launch-claims gate, which consumes this file; until it
exists, regeneration is manual and marked.

## Ground truth

Classification is against the ratified launch profile (`scripts/launch-profile.mjs`,
DR-005): **launch** = grounded in a launch-profile entry, a `pnpm run proof:*`
command that exists, or a served OpenAPI path (the grounding is cited and was
verified); **deferred** = the capability exists in the tree but is outside the
ratified launch scope; **demo-only** = true of a demo-classified surface only;
**unsubstantiated** = no artifact in the tree backs it. Every non-launch row
cites its contradicting artifact.

## The headline numbers

**592 claims** across 18 files: 148 launch,
208 deferred, 69 demo-only,
167 unsubstantiated. Actions owed by row 6:
**136 remove**, **255 rewrite**, 201 keep.

## The dimension-count conflict, resolved

Three different counts of what SignalGrid evaluates today are published:
**six** (README.md:3, Pricing.tsx:16), **five** (docs/WHAT_SIGNALGRID_DOES_TODAY.md:80,
:169-170), **three** (the ratified launch profile, scripts/launch-profile.mjs:243-252).
The one defensible public number is **three** — device posture, device-management
health, and local authority — because it is the only count with a ratifying
decision record and an enforcing gate. README.md:3, Pricing.tsx:16 and both
WHAT_SIGNALGRID_DOES_TODAY.md lines must move to it together (rows below).

## Honesty note on review depth

The completeness critic fully re-audited README.md and Pricing.tsx (every
launch grounding executed or opened; three misclassifications found and
corrected in this file), re-verified the Federal.tsx hot rows directly against
`docs/SECURITY_BASELINE_ALIGNMENT.md`, and recovered 16 missed claims. The
remaining surfaces carry single-pass extraction; row 6's gate build should
re-verify any row it acts on.
